# Graph Report - himmel  (2026-11-07)

## Corpus Check
- 5154 files · ~9,455,942 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 1029 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .jsonl 230, .out 135, .patch 106)

## Summary
- 49245 nodes · 93906 edges · 3362 communities (2223 shown, 1139 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3171 edges (avg confidence: 0.79)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `d9e77b24`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
